### Required acceptance coverage and task status

- [ ] Extraction/source checks: exact text bindings/offsets, original mentions, labeled repairs, time roles/precision, depth/access qualifiers, native-only groups, negation/forecast/clearance and malformed/tampered results.
- [ ] Geometry checks: real positive placement, ambiguous/missing-locality/asset rejection, retained NOAH gaps and existing corridor policy/provenance.
- [ ] Persistence checks: complete evidence relationships; archive-only inactive insertion versus explicit active-demo activation; transaction rollback; duplicate/concurrent/retry handling; original-versus-demo ownership; deactivation-to-history lifecycle and unchanged unrelated data/routing.
- [ ] History/analytics checks: source dates versus verification dates, date-only/unknown cases, sourced/assumed/unknown closure, separate simulated duration, and card/map/filter/pagination/export parity.
- [ ] UI checks: native/legacy output, articles without cards, errors/Retry/stale content, automatic refresh, focus/keyboard/touch and desktop/mobile overflow.
- [ ] Cloud checks: approved migration compatibility, actual model/isolation/resource qualification, protected saved-data GETs, exact deployed identities and cleanup.

**Current task status:** all eight Phase 1 tasks are complete locally: 434 distinct backend checks, 45 distinct desktop/mobile project checks, TypeScript, focused lint and production build pass. Three browser cases are intentional skips. [Indexed evidence](evaluations/news-historical-phase1-20261022/README.md). E0/E1 is preserved. Phase 2 deployment, shared-output qualification, actual September execution and hosted manual checks remain open. No hosted manual checkbox is passed by these fictional fixture checks.

**Status: Phase A, local Phase B, schema prerequisites and controlled local Phase C acceptance are complete; Phase D monitoring contracts and the local E1.1 maintenance correction are verified.** The objective remains automatic RSS-to-NER processing and saved results in the existing `/admin/news` interface. C3b connects RSS admission/corrections, original feed provenance, native readers and downstream gates in the guarded disposable-local pipeline. **754 distinct tests pass**, with one real ten-input run matching 4,483 spans/all 53 reviewed predictions; peak 2,492.09 MiB. [C3b report](evaluations/rss-ner-rss-integration-20261021/README.md). Eight inputs enter through mocked RSS; two short pre-existing capture controls correctly fail RSS admission and exercise processing separately. The frontend and scheduled production activation have not shipped. The historical presentation requirements and detailed E0–E6 implementation plan are recorded above; E0 preparation, E1.1 and E1.2 are complete locally; remaining historical/cloud qualification and release gates are open.

[Report and cloud retry disposition](evaluations/rss-ner-monitoring-20261021/README.md). The later review found invalid auditor configuration can prevent independent publication/expiry maintenance; that correction now passes 82 checks locally in [E1.1](evaluations/news-maintenance-e11-20261022/README.md), with cloud release still pending. Original collection diagnostics for the dated retry were not retained; no underlying publisher recovery is claimed. **Next implementation action: the explicit historical execution-target policy in E1**, followed by remaining date/source/geometry and E1–E6 gates. E0, E1.1 and E1.2 are complete locally; cloud processing and real September inference have not run. Phase F investigates 403s last. Shared migration, qualified cloud resources, production selection/deployment and actual Cloud Run verification remain release gates. Local acceptance does not qualify the unchanged last-inspected one-GiB cloud job; historical runtime failures remain valid.

**Owner of every phase, task and gate below:** [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe). The user approved this roadmap on October 20, 2026, the original Phase B plan, and separately the local two-GiB and three-GiB follow-ups. Each profile receives one frozen attempt. The user separately approved the minimal SQLAlchemy/migration change and disposable local verification, now delivered. That authorization does not cover shared/cloud migration, cloud resources or production deployment. Keep gate and implementation checkboxes open until the corresponding evidence exists.

**Target workflow:** adaptive cloud scheduling → RSS discovery → article shortlisting → full-body capture → trained NER locations → qualified location repairs → depth/time/status/access grouping → evidence and placement checks → saved results → existing publication workflow and admin interface. Preserve the current durable extraction-before-evaluation transaction boundary when implementing this logical flow; the UI must not trigger collection or inference by reading saved results.

### Agreed boundaries and decision controls

- Preserve trained **v19 control seed 0** and qualified **v40 → v44 combined → v42 grouping** behavior. DEPTH/TIME recognition and grouping remain rule based; do not describe them as learned NER capabilities.
- Preserve original neural predictions separately from effective mentions and labeled rule repairs. Retain exact source offsets, evidence, qualifiers, temporal roles, collective scope, uncertainty and unassigned facts. Never borrow a broader location's facts to complete a road.
- Retain existing publisher, locality, body-completeness, actual-flood-evidence, publication, geometry, freshness, conflict, expiry and vehicle-policy gates. Recognition alone cannot establish current flooding, verified geometry or routing eligibility.
- Preserve observation, onset, report, publication, recession and clearance meanings. Subsided evidence remains available in history without becoming current plotted flooding; receding water does not itself establish clearance.
- No retraining, new dataset collection, reference revisions, validation/holdout reuse, further October flood-event research or unrelated interface improvements. Normal approved RSS collection is the intended integration target; the dataset-collection exclusion is not an instruction to disable the collector.
- Keep the 11 unresolved reviewed-article rows, three reference holds, type-family real-evidence limitation, non-flood access omission and language/training limitations explicit. Do not impose another research/training cycle before interface work or silently call those limitations resolved.
- Database model/migration changes require a separate concrete proposal and explicit human approval. Production deployment is a later release gate. Until then, retain the existing default-disabled local preview and serving production behavior.
- Work in order: **A contract → B runtime qualification → C automatic processing → D monitoring corrections → E interface acceptance → F 403 investigation → release decision**. A failed gate produces a documented blocker and concrete follow-up within that phase, not an unrelated research detour. Implementation records must name the requirement advanced and the evidence for completion.

### Phase A — Establish the integration contract

- [x] Trace RSS metadata filtering, retrieval, body-based admission, immutable capture, extraction, placement, evaluation, publication and admin readers. Record the actual call chain and all relevant consumers before changing wiring.

- [x] Specify durable V46 output: original neural mentions, effective mentions, ordered repairs, grouped facts, unresolved facts/reasons, source identity, and model/tokenizer/rule provenance. Bind exact Unicode source offsets to the unchanged captured body; title/excerpt offsets must not be treated as body offsets.
- [x] Specify compatibility with existing `ExtractedClaim` consumers, including joint intersections, collective measurements, ranges, repeated names, LOCAL_AREA mentions and incomplete targets. Do not flatten shared evidence into invented individual depths or discard facts that cannot be projected safely.
- [x] Define how saved runs and the admin distinguish legacy rules-only output from NER-backed output, and how existing historical runs remain readable.
- [x] Prepare the smallest necessary schema/interface proposal, with compatibility and migration verification requirements, for explicit approval before any SQLAlchemy model or Alembic edit.

At contract delivery the extraction-run constraint accepted only `rules_only`; the separately approved schema step now also permits explicit `ner_rules_v1`. - [ ] Keep cards focused on location, reported depth, condition, publisher and observation time. Add compact severity, observation-age and passability indicators using backend-provided meaning and existing shared styling.
- [ ] Preserve Unknown/Unassessed for insufficient evidence. Observation age, severity and publisher identity must not imply that a road is currently safe or flooded; keep observation and publication clocks distinct.
- [ ] Expose original neural predictions, labeled rule repairs, grouped facts and unresolved facts with reasons/supporting evidence inside existing detail views. Retain the article link and distinguish new NER output from legacy processing history.
- [ ] Keep technical hashes, versions and diagnostics in existing detailed views; no unrelated diagnostics redesign or unannotated optional improvements.

**Automatic saved-data updates — normal Refresh controls removed**

- [ ] Remove normal Refresh buttons throughout this News Intelligence workflow, including history/monitoring refresh controls. Retain Retry actions for failed requests; do not add Collect now.
- [ ] Automatically reload saved information when relevant views open, filters change, focus returns or a completed worker cycle invalidates their data. Reuse the status heartbeat and query cache; avoid duplicate polling and stop unnecessary polling while the document is hidden.
- [ ] Preserve filters, pagination, focus and open details during updates. Surface stale-data/error states while retaining previously loaded content. Reading a view must never initiate collection, live source probes, inference or publication.
- [ ] Run desktop/mobile browser acceptance for cards, articles without cards, article/evidence inspection, unresolved facts, source selection/health, automatic updates, errors, keyboard use and overflow.

**Gate E:** the existing page displays saved NER-backed results through consistent shared components; normal Refresh controls are absent, automatic reads work, Retry remains available on failure, and both screen sizes pass acceptance. No extra screen, manual collection control or unrelated UI improvement is included.

### Phase F — Investigate HTTP 403 errors last

Begin detailed 403 investigation only after the preceding integration and interface checks; do not allow it to displace the agreed primary work.

- [ ] Capture the exact failing URL, response, timestamp and affected stage. Distinguish publisher/feed denial, LANES authorization failure and map-provider failure, including map requests made by the persistent admin map.
- [ ] Establish whether each error blocks discovery, article-body retrieval, extraction, publication or only map rendering. Keep saved audit evidence separate from current observed requests; do not infer the user's exact error from historical Inquirer or MapTiler failures.
- [ ] Apply only evidence-supported fixes while preserving publisher access restrictions and application authorization. Do not bypass access challenges, weaken authorization or treat feed excerpts as complete reporting bodies.
- [ ] Verify recovery, or record an external blocker, affected coverage and next responsible action. A publisher block may remain external; it must not be hidden or classified as an empty successful article.

**Gate F:** every investigated 403 has an origin, operational effect and verified fix or explicit external-blocker disposition. Do not expand into unrelated infrastructure work without recording the specific requirement and evidence.

### Completion and release decision

- [ ] NER participates in automatic article processing; saved results preserve evidence, provenance, uncertainty, unresolved facts and previous extraction runs.
- [ ] Existing News Intelligence views display those saved results using shared components; accepted monitoring fixes and automatic reads pass targeted backend and desktop/mobile tests.
- [ ] Reverify the **60/15/30** collection policy and distinguish controller heartbeat, collection attempts, pipeline outcomes and final Cloud Run execution status.
- [ ] Record explicit dispositions for the cloud attempt-level finding and Phase F errors, including any external blockers and their effect on release readiness.
- [ ] Complete any separately approved schema migration checks, including `alembic upgrade head` in the intended controlled environment, dependency synchronization and updated schema documentation before release work.
- [ ] Prepare matching API/worker/frontend release artifacts, authenticated model-asset delivery, deployment verification and rollback instructions for a separate production release decision. No cloud mutation, push or deployment is authorized merely by this roadmap.
- [ ] As each phase is actually delivered, record its evidence, update this checklist and the reverse-chronological progress record, and synchronize affected system/stack/roadmap/issue documents. Preserve frozen research reports and original completed milestones; a documentation update alone does not mean integration shipped.

**Earlier checkpoints below are historical.** Their recorded measurements and completion states remain intact. Older unchecked next steps are historical proposals unless explicitly carried into this current milestone; use the ordered phases above rather than restarting superseded studies.

## Current NER scope and defense checkpoint

**Current-scope reconciliation — October 20:** use the [RSS-to-NER integration milestone](#current-milestone-rss-to-ner-integration-and-news-intelligence) for execution order. V46 is complete for the local backend preview; automatic durable NER processing and the admin interface remain pending. The v34 result and initial defense-priority preparation below are historical checkpoints, not the current repair gate or an instruction to repeat completed studies. The original learned DEPTH/TIME objective remains unfinished and outside this no-retraining integration milestone. [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

**Original user goal remains open:** train recognition of **locations, DEPTH and TIME expressions**, then correctly associate each location with its supported depth, condition, access and time. Several locations can share a supported depth/time; different depths must not leak between roads. Observation time is not automatically onset, and receding water is not automatically clearance. This requirement comes from the user; it is not conditional on an adviser request.

| Capability | Actual status |
|---|---|
| Trained location recognition | V19 control seed 0 learns ROAD, LANDMARK, LOCAL_AREA, BARANGAY, CITY and PROVINCE. Linux qualification and offline location comparison are complete. |
| Trained DEPTH recognition | **Not trained in the current neural model.** Existing depth rules are separate. |
| Trained TIME recognition | **Not trained in the current neural model.** Existing time extraction/resolution rules are separate. |
| Correct per-location/shared facts | Qualified v40/v44/v42 behavior is connected to the V46 local preview; unresolved facts and documented limitations remain. Automatic durable claim extraction is not yet connected. |
| Complete automatic system | Neural integration, isolated end-to-end acceptance and operational release remain separate unfinished gates. |

**Score interpretation:** existing NER F1 scores measure the six location labels, not DEPTH/TIME or full flood-information extraction. The completed 14-input comparison verifies runtime behavior and disagreements, not accuracy.

**Sunday, October 25, 2026 defense — interim priority selected:** the user chose a limited demonstration using the trained location model plus depth/time rules. Prioritize its bounded offline acceptance before a read-only LANES connection; no additional neural training runs in this preparation path. Learned DEPTH/TIME remains an unfinished later annotation/training/evaluation requirement, not cancelled or fulfilled by rules. No complete-system or October 25 readiness promise follows. [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

**Existing-data review completed:** all 405 records are inventoried; 26 exact contextual pilot counterparts exist, all in training. Five are held and 21 support limited diagnostics; 379 records, including all 60 validation passages, lack matching contextual references. One guarded rules pass completed on all 405 passages; no neural inference or training ran. **659 fresh tests pass, including 48 new checks.** [Gap assessment](evaluations/flood-ner-context-gaps-20261019/README.md).

**Interim acceptance package prepared:** all 26 training counterparts have source-bound proposals: **20 retain / four corrected / two held**. The 24 eligible proposals contain 35 DEPTH / 22 TIME spans; unknown/unassigned associations remain explicit. **40 fictional cases** and a fixed behavior contract are frozen; **753 fresh regressions pass (94 new)** and guarded CPU schema/tokenizer qualification passes. No extraction repair or neural inference occurred. [Outcome](evaluations/flood-ner-context-acceptance-20261019/README.md).

**Source-use clarification retained at the developer's request:** The project assembled the dataset; manual collection does not transfer rights in the underlying news wording. Original human selection/arrangement, annotations and source text have distinct rights questions; current labels retain their AI silver provenance. “No dataset license granted” means no public reuse license covering the collected news text has been established, not that a generic dataset license must be purchased. “Training admission” is an internal LANES readiness status, not a government permit. Document the applicable use basis separately for academic training/evaluation, paper excerpts, dataset distribution, production use and model distribution: existing permission, rights-holder authorization or an applicable exception assessed for that use. Academic purpose and attribution alone do not establish fair use. This acknowledgement records no permission or admission decision. [IP Code, Sections 173–175](https://www.wipo.int/wipolex/en/legislation/details/18399); [IPOPHL fair-use guidance](https://www.ipophil.gov.ph/news/ipophl-releases-statutory-fair-use-guidelines-to-clarify-rules-on-copyright-exceptions/). [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

| --- | --- |

**Next implementation: forecast-to-Unconfirmed with retained public updates.** Complete this before claiming automatic ML expiry. [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

1. Define the server-owned forecast trigger, eligible evidence versus simulation policy, uncertainty handling and settings interaction. Keep a stable source/model/version identity; preserve explicit staff deadlines and require newer qualified evidence to refresh a decision.
2. Implement and audit idempotent Active → Unconfirmed transitions. Forecast expiry must never manufacture Cleared, observed dry timestamps or training labels.
3. Retain Unconfirmed zones in public snapshots/map/badge within the retention policy. Extend the existing citizen update and Active Zone admin-review flow to retained zones; keep the map bubble's original content and Moderation Center's report-outcome scope.
4. Implement the user's choice about uncertain roads through explicit vehicle-aware routing behavior. Test public visibility, update eligibility, stale/concurrent decisions, settings changes and refresh/reload behavior on desktop/mobile.
5. Keep independent outcome/location-transfer validation, expansion of the distinct news-placement coverage, matching API/frontend release and physical PWA acceptance open. Local simulations are not production qualification.

**Current two-limit delivery:** location coverage is completed; feature capture and comparisons are completed, but replacing the shared baseline with a demonstrably better street-specific model is **still open**. No operational forecast-to-Unconfirmed/routing change or deployment is part of this delivery. [Plan](plans/pasig-location-and-feature-duration.md), [evaluation](evaluations/pasig-location-feature-models-20261008/README.md). [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

**Selected data path:** build the independent dataset through future LANES reports and admin reviews. Automatic source-input snapshots now include public condition updates and timed owner follow-ups; a bounded private reviewed model-evidence export is implemented. Future real episodes and qualification still need to accumulate; no generated dry labels, clock backfill or automatic retraining. [@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe)

**Scope alignment (October 4):** Continue Metro Manila-wide news collection and automatic flood plotting. Pasig City is the primary thesis evaluation area for the AI, plotting, and automatic expiry features. Do not imply that NCR-wide collection means expiry has been validated across every city. Finish and evaluate the system features before revising the research document; see the [duration and lifecycle plan](plans/flood-evidence-lifecycle-and-duration-plan.md).

**Decision record:** [Decision 23](decisions.md#23-metro-manila-product-coverage-pasig-duration-study-and-separated-evidence-datasets) consolidates geography, separated evidence CSVs and the conditional model gate. [Decision 22](decisions.md#22-separate-observed-flood-status-evidence-expiry-and-predicted-clearance) defines Active/Unconfirmed/Cleared. Working and conditional reported-subsidence proxy exports are delivered; [Decision 24](decisions.md#24-purpose-specific-reported-subsidence-proxy-and-prospective-prediction-reference) adopts the target/reference policy. A conditional experimental AFT baseline is now fitted; prospective training admission and production model selection remain pending. The source-alert two-hour evidence lifecycle is implemented locally. Updating status from reports does not automatically train a model.

**Strict collection follow-up released:** v4 requires affirmative local reporting-body flood evidence and admits no new unreadable candidates. Previously saved unsupported articles use an excluded-history filter outside default attention. All 341 backend tests, 15 PostgreSQL checks, TypeScript and 15 deployed desktop/mobile cases pass (API/session mocked). Matching API/collector/Firebase releases, ten new preserved-history results and the normal collector execution succeeded. All 24 saved articles are excluded; no current local flood is established by this set. No model, migration or dependency changes. Resume the original automatic OSM/NOAH/DRRMO integration. > **Metro Manila scope decision:** RSS discovery, article processing, predicted placement, and news-derived zones target Metro Manila, including Pasig. The collector prioritizes flood headlines with Metro Manila place clues, and can probe up to five additional articles per run whose location is absent from the feed metadata. Those extra articles need a body-grounded local flood claim; blocked, out-of-scope, future-dated, and budget-limited entries produce run notices. The source revision is not deployed. The six approved feeds are still polled because they are publisher-wide feeds. Nationwide PSGC extraction code remains in the repository as completed historical work but is outside this phase's active evaluation and rollout. > The three-article check was an exploratory diagnostic, not an RSS replay or an acceptance test. Work through these gates before claiming that news can safely create live map zones. The deployed collector uses Cloud SQL; development tests use fixtures or a disposable development database, never production rows. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

> **Automatic operation is the intended outcome.** A credible current report should reach commuters without a routine staff queue. Staff review is reserved for conflicting or incomplete evidence. Publish an immediate source-labeled news alert when the affected segment is unresolved; promote it to a routing-affecting zone automatically once a bounded segment and the evidence gates are met. The Pasig DRRMO CSV supplies year, place, landmark, and estimated depth context, but no event date, observation/clearance time, coordinates, or confirmed distinct-event IDs. Its repeated rows are not proven independent flood events. LiPAD/UP NOAH layers are modeled susceptibility, not live observations. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

#### 3. Taglish NLP Extraction and Normalization (Pasig baseline completed; Metro Manila rollout pending)

> **Delivered scope and owner:** The completed extractor is a Pasig-focused baseline for evidence-linked, structured flood facts, later extended with nationwide PSGC references. It does not choose map geometry, create official zones, or change routing. The [cleaned DRRMO CSV](../data/flooded_areas_pasig_clean.csv) is a Pasig location-ranking prior, not NER training text. The active rollout and remaining evaluation target Metro Manila. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

**3.1 Model feasibility and reproducible runtime**

- [x] Check calamanCy's package and model licenses (MIT for toolkit/model), supported spaCy versions (`>=3.8.3` requiring PyTorch and `spacy-transformers`, conflicting with pinned `spacy==3.7.5`), 203.7 MB wheel size, and coarse Tagalog NER labels (`LOC`, `ORG`, `PER` without flood attributes). Feasible CPU rule-based extraction baseline established per Section 3.1 instructions. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Run a CPU-only spike and feasibility analysis: Documented that `tl_calamancy_md` pulls 350+ MB of PyTorch/Transformers dependencies and lacks depth/condition labels. Built a deterministic, explainable, lightweight rule/gazetteer and regex extraction engine running at 0.71 ms/item on CPU with zero heavy dependencies. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

**3.2 Input contract and evaluation set**

- [x] Define a read-only extractor input from persisted `news_articles`: article ID, canonical URL, publisher, title, excerpt, `article_text`, publication time, and fetch time. Process full text only when capture succeeded; keep metadata-only leads visible for staff without claiming full-text extraction. Preserve original text, offsets, and source timestamps via `NewsArticleExtractorInput`. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Assemble an initial 50-item, manually labeled evaluation set in `data/taglish_flood_eval_set.json` spanning Filipino/English/Taglish, positive and negative flood mentions, negation, forecasts, historical reports, aliases, ambiguous places, multiple locations, and absent depth/time. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Build automated evaluation runner in `backend/scripts/evaluate_taglish_extraction.py` recording accuracy across place span, canonical barangay recall (100%), canonical depth match (100%), condition match (100%), and CPU latency (0.71 ms/item). ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

**3.3 Place and flood-fact extraction**

- [x] Combine deterministic Pasig rules/gazetteer matches for city, barangay, road/street, and landmark names; retain the original mention and character offsets. Use [PSGC-backed Pasig barangays](../data/pasig_barangay_reference.csv) to normalize “Brgy.”/“Barangay,” “Sta.”/“Santa,” and known spelling variants. Leave conflicting or out-of-Pasig matches unresolved for later ranking or staff review. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Extract flood mentions and supporting sentences; associate each depth, condition, place, and time with the flood statement it describes. Handle multiple incidents/locations in one article without treating every named place as flooded. Distinguish an observed flood from a warning, forecast, historical reference, quoted denial, or negated report. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Map explicit depth language and measurements to the existing canonical depth keys (`gutter`, `half-knee`, `half-tire`, `knee`, `tires`, `waist`, `chest`, `neck`) only where an explainable rule supports the match. Keep ranges, vague phrases, contradictory values, or unmatched units unknown; record the original phrase and rule used. Integrated with Phase 37 single-source-of-truth MMDA physical depth registry (`flood_depth.py` / `floodDepth.ts`), ensuring claims bridge vernacular Taglish/cm/inch terms with exact metric (`0.20m`–`1.52m`) and imperial (`8"`–`60"+`) measurements without altering DB tables. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Extract condition as `active`, `rising`, `receding`, `subsided`, or `unknown`, independent of moderation state (`pending`, `approved`, `rejected`). Extract explicit event dates/times and cautiously resolve relative phrases using the article's publication time and Asia/Manila context; keep uncertain time unknown and never substitute fetch/publication time as an observed event time. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

- [x] Define a versioned backend extraction result `NewsExtractionResult` with per-claim raw span, normalized value or unknown, evidence sentence/offset, source article ID/URL, rule or model provenance, and uncertainty reason. Keep separate claims when an article reports different places or times. Make reruns deterministic and idempotent. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Implement extraction service in `backend/app/services/taglish_extraction_service.py` as a server-side, staff-only dry run or batch step over persisted candidates, with surfaced extraction errors and no public-map or routing side effect. Zero new third-party dependencies required. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Pass offline regression tests in `backend/tests/test_taglish_extraction.py` for aliases, depth/status phrases, negation, multiple places, missing full text, time ambiguity, provenance, and repeat processing (11/11 passing, 27/27 total backend discovery & extraction tests passing). ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))

#### 7. Definition of Done

- [ ] Staff can discover a deduplicated Metro Manila flood candidate from an approved source, inspect preserved evidence, extracted facts, and physical depth measurements (`depth_formatted`), see explainable location suggestions across Metro Manila, and correct exceptions through the existing verified-zone workflow.
- [ ] Smart Auto-Activation Gate: Validate observed-event time, exact place evidence, road geometry, canonical depth, and independently confirmed active condition before connecting article processing to zone creation. Auditor failure must not authorize activation; score thresholds require calibration on real articles. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] Strict Safety Suppression: Reports of subsided water ("humupa na") and forecasts ("posibleng bahain") are strictly suppressed with zero created avoidance zones, keeping passable roads open. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [x] OSM placement connection: explicit spans can retain supported centerlines and broad names retain ambiguous bounded candidates. Legacy 50m buffering is a preview utility; it does not establish observed flooded width or satisfy operational-zone acceptance. ([@roicambe](https://github.com/roicambe) (Roi Cambe))
- [ ] Tests cover sources and flood locations from multiple Metro Manila cities, same-name place ambiguity, missing historical/hazard priors, filtering/deduplication, extraction, authorization, and staff review for ambiguous candidates. Desktop and mobile review must show and allow editing suggested geometry outside the Pasig border within Metro Manila without a Pasig-only geocoding bias.
- [ ] Document any dependency, model, migration, endpoint, or UI added during implementation.
